   L=n-1; length=2*L+1

   A=np.zeros(n); B=np.zeros(n); W=np.zeros(n)
   thresh = 1e-14 maybe

      if pk <= thresh: continue
      k = idx - L
      # first term valid r-k? For c_k formula, W range: r-k valid -> r in [max(0,k), min(n,n+k))
      lo = k if k>0 else 0

      # But if k positive, hi=min(n,n+k)=n+k (<n); if k negative, hi=n.

          # B: r+k = u valid, r=u-k. u range lo:hi? For B term -h_{r+k}; r+k=u, r = u-k, same r range lo:hi. h at u from lo:hi.

          ahi = n-k if k<0 else n
          A[alo+k:ahi+k] += pk*x[alo:ahi]

Updating V after swap:
```
def apply_swap_binary(h,V,f,i,j,n,L):
   # i zero->1, j one->0
   # delta = V[i] - V[j]; adjust cross in f?
   delta = V[i] - V[j]
   f_new = f + delta
   f_new[L] -= 2
   idx_pos = L + j - i
   idx_neg = L + i - j
   f_new[idx_pos] += 1
   f_new[idx_neg] += 1
   # update h
   h[i]=1; h[j]=0
   # update V for q=i δ=+1, q=j δ=-1
   p=np.arange(n)
   idx1=L + i - p; V[p, idx1] -= 1
   idx2=L + p - i; V[p, idx2] -= 1
   idx1=L + j - p; V[p, idx1] += 1
   idx2=L + p - j; V[p, idx2] += 1
   return f_new
```

Implement continuous_refine:
```
def continuous_refine(h_start, n, deadline, seed, alphas=None):
    h = project(h_start,n)
    best_c = eval_exact(h,n); best_h=h
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    if alphas is None: alphas=[20,50,100,300,1000,3000]
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'jac':sum_constraint_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    for start_idx in range(n_starts): # maybe 3
       if time>deadline break
       if start_idx==0: x0=best_h
       else: x0=project(best_h + scale*rng.randn(n), n) with scale [0.02,0.05]
       for alpha in alphas:
           if time>deadline break
           maxiter = 80 if n<=300 else 50
           res = minimize(smooth_fun, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=smooth_jac, args=(n,alpha), options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
           x=res.x
           x=project(x,n)
           c=eval_exact(x,n)
           if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=x
           x0=x
    # active gradient polish
    x0=best_h
    for steps in active_projected(...): update
    return best_h,best_c
```

In weighted_gradient, threshold maybe `1e-12`. For p length maybe 2n-1. Use local variables for speed. Code:
```
def weighted_gradient(p, x, n, dx):
    L=n-1
    W=np.zeros(n); A=np.zeros(n); B=np.zeros(n)
    # iterate over nonzeros
    for idx in np.nonzero(p > 1e-13)[0]:
       pk=float(p[idx])
       if pk <= 1e-13: continue
       k=idx-L
       if k >= 0:
           if k < n:
              lo=k; hi=n-k # hi for B? for A hiA=n, hiB=n-k
              # A[k:n] += pk*x[0:n-k]
              cnt=n-k
              if cnt>0:
                 W[k:n] += pk
                 A[k:n] += pk*x[:cnt]
                 B[:cnt] += pk*x[k:]
       else:
           m=-k
           cnt=n-m
           if cnt>0:
              W[:cnt] += pk
              A[:cnt] += pk*x[m:]
              B[m:] += pk*x[:cnt]
    return dx*(W-A-B)
```
Check for k=0: cnt=n, W[:n]+=pk; A[:n]+=pk*x[:n]; B[:n? x[0:]? For k>=0 branch: cnt=n, B[:cnt]+=pk*x[0:n], yes.
For k positive near L=n-1: cnt=1; W[n-1:n]+=pk; A[n-1:n]+=pk*x[:1]; B[:1]+=pk*x[n-1:]. Good.
For k negative near -L: cnt=1; W[:1]+=pk; A[:1]+=pk*x[n-1:]; B[n-1:]+=pk*x[:1]. Good.

We can speed by precomputing nonzero p indices. But p changes. Use loop over enumerate; length ≤999. In Python, okay.

Smooth_fun:
```
def smooth_fun(x,n,alpha):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    q=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    M=q.max()
    z=alpha*(q-M)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return float(M*2/n)
    return float((M + np.log(s)/alpha)*2/n)
```
Potential q contains NaNs if x NaN; return inf.

Smooth_jac:
```
def smooth_jac(x,n,alpha):
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return np.zeros(n)
   q=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
   M=q.max(); z=alpha*(q-M); ez=np.exp(z); s=ez.sum()
   if s<=0: p=np.zeros; p[q==M]=1/count
   else: p=ez/s
   # maybe normalize?
   return weighted_gradient(p,x,n,2.0/n)
```

SLSQP asked jac shape (n,), okay.

Active gradient polish:
```
def active_polish(h,n,deadline,max_steps=2000, tol=1e-6):
   h=project(h,n); best=eval_exact; step initial maybe 1e-3? Use backtracking with exact C. Need gradient for active top K.
   for it in range(max_steps):

def binary_start_from_h(h,n,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # choose top N by value + small noise
    vals = h + 1e-4*rng.randn(n)
    idx = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]

Implementation of vectorized first-improvement:
```
def binary_sweep_once(h,V,f,M,n,L, zeros, ones, use_full, rng, max_i? ):
   Oarr = np.asarray(ones, dtype=np.intp)
   row_idx = np.arange(len(Oarr))
   improved=False
   # order zeros maybe shuffled
   for i in zeros_order:
      if time>deadline: break
      base = np.add(f, V[i]) # new int32
      # If len(ones) large, cand memory. We can process ones in chunks to limit memory.
      # chunk size e.g. 64
      for cs in range(0,lenO,chunk):
          Os = Oarr[cs:cs+chunk]
          cand = base[None,:] - V[Os]
          cand[:, L] -= 2
          cand[np.arange(len(Os)), L + Os - i] += 1
          cand[np.arange(len(Os)), L + i - Os] += 1
          rowmax = cand.max(axis=1)
          # find local min
          pos = np.argmin(rowmax)
          nm = rowmax[pos]
          if nm < M:
              j = int(Os[pos])
              # apply swap
              f = f + V[i] - V[j]; f[L]-=2; f[L+j-i]+=1; f[L+i-j]+=1
              M = int(nm)
              h[i]=1; h[j]=0
              update V
              zeros_order = remove i add j? easiest rebuild after accept
              return True, h,V,f,M
      # no improvement in chunk from this i
   return False, ...
```
If we modify cand in place, no alias issue. chunk size tune: 64 or 128. For n=500, lenO=250; chunk 250 one matrix. For n=1000, lenO=500; chunk 64 -> 8 matrices of 64x1999 ~0.5MB, okay.

def binary_refine(h_start, n, time_limit, seed, max_starts=...):
    t_start=time.time(); deadline=t_start+time_limit
    best_h=None; best_M=n//2+1
    base_h = binary_start_from_h(h_start,n,seed)
    # list of starts: [base, several permutations/patterns]
    starts = [base]
    for k in range(5): starts.append(random_binary(...))
    for pattern... starts.append(...)
    for si, sb in enumerate(starts):
       if time>deadline break
       # time per start depending n: min(remaining, 30?)
       h,V,f,M = init_binary(sb,n)
       if M<best_M: update
       # local improve loop with restart perturbations
       local_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + max(time_limit*0.15, 20))? 
       perturbations=0
       while time.time() < local_deadline:
           improved_any=False
           # try reduce M, max_iter maybe 200
           while time.time() < local_deadline:
               zeros=where h==0; ones=where h==1
               if len(zeros)==0: break
               rng.shuffle(zeros)
               # possibly limit zeros scanned if time tight
               ok = binary_sweep_once(..., deadline=local_deadline)
               if not ok: break
               improved_any=True
               update best
           # if local opt and time left, perturb
           perturb_count = rng.randint(3,8)
           for _ in range(perturb_count):
              zeros=...; ones=...
              i=rng.choice(zeros); j=rng.choice(ones)
              apply_swap...
           # continue (new local improvement)
       update best
```

Let's think about exact evaluation of continuous h with SLSQP. If h has many tiny fractional values, `np.correlate` maybe exact enough. Need final projection: `project_feasible` should make sum as close to n/2 as possible and clip. For binary, exact. For continuous, if SLSQP slightly violates sum, project. But projection may change objective. We can implement water-filling exactly:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
   N=n/2.0
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   for _ in range(100):
      s=h.sum()
      diff=N-s
      if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
      if diff>0:
         # add to values <1 proportional to room? Uniform to room maybe
         room=1-h
         rsum=room.sum()
         if rsum<1e-12: break
         h=h+diff*room/rsum
      else:
         h=h+diff*(h/h.sum())
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # final exact adjustment: distribute residual over interior points with max/min step
   diff=N-h.sum()
   if diff>0:
      idx=np.flatnonzero(h<1) sorted by maybe fractional
      for idx: add=min(diff,1-h[idx]); if diff small break
   else: subtract.
   return h
```
This may leave sum at floating residual ~1e-16. Good.

`eval_exact` top-level:
```
def eval_exact(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n: return np.inf
    if np.any(h < -1e-9) or np.any(h > 1+1e-9): 
       h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))
```
To avoid modifying input? fine.

`project_feasible` as above. Need N may be int or .5. For n even int. Use float.

`make_binary_from_float`:
```
def make_binary_from_float(hf, n, seed=0):
    N = n//2
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    hf = np.asarray(hf, float)
    if hf.size != n: hf = np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,n), np.linspace(0,2,2),? ) # not needed
    tie = 1e-6 * rng.random_sample(n)
    order = np.argsort(hf + tie)[::-1]
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    b[order[:N]] = 1
    if b.sum() != N: adjust (should)
    return b
```
For even n, N int.

Vectorized sweep function:
```
def binary_sweep_once(h,V,f,M,n,L, deadline, rng, chunk_size=64):
    zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
    ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
    if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: return False, None
    rng.shuffle(zeros)
    os = ones  # array
    row_idx_full = np.arange(len(os))
    accepted_pair = None
    pos_arr = np.arange(len(os))
    for i in zeros:
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       base = f + V[i].astype(np.int32)  # V int8, f int32 -> int32? f+V[i] yields int32 if V int8? It may upcast to int32.
       # If base has M+? okay
       # chunk process ones
       for start in range(0, len(os), chunk_size):
           idx = slice(start, start+chunk_size)
           Os = os[idx]
           if Os.size == 0: continue
           # If many, maybe skip if i has no chance? no.
           cand = base[None, :] - V[Os].astype(np.int32)
           cand[:, L] -= 2
           rp = np.arange(Os.size)
           ipos = L + Os - i
           cand[rp, ipos] += 1
           ineg = L + i - Os
          cand[rp, ineg] += 1
           rowmax = cand.max(axis=1)
           # Find any < M, choose minimum for this i
           # if M small, rowmax maybe int32
           minidx = int(np.argmin(rowmax))
           nm = int(rowmax[minidx])
           if nm < M:
               j = int(Os[minidx])
               # apply
               f_new, M_new = apply_binary_swap(h,V,f,i,j,n,L)
               # update best? M_new should nm, due numerical maybe
               return True, (i,j,f_new,M_new)
    return False, None
```

Local improvement loop with perturbations:
```
def binary_local_optimize(h,V,f,M,n,L,deadline, rng, max_perturbs, chunk):
    while time.time() < deadline:
        improved_any=False
        # reduction loop
        while time.time() < deadline:
            res = binary_sweep_once(...)
            if res is None or not res[0]: break
            f,M = res[2], res[3]
            improved_any=True
            if M <= some? no
        # update best outside
        if time.time() >= deadline: break
        if max_perturbs <=0: break
        max_perturbs -=1
        # perturb
        zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=...; if none break
        k = rng.randint(3,8)
        for _ in range(k):
            zeros=...; ones=...
            i=rng.choice(zeros); j=rng.choice(ones)
            f,M=apply_binary_swap(...)
        # continue
    return h,V,f,M
```

def binary_refine(h_start,n,time_budget,seed):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+time_budget
    best_h=None; best_M=10**9; best_c=np.inf
    N=n//2

    # create initial binary from h_start, and a handful variants
    starts=[]

    starts.append(b0)
    # threshold with noise scales 1e-3,1e-2
    for sc in [1e-3,1e-2,5e-2]:
       bf = project_feasible(np.asarray(h_start,float)+sc*rng.randn(n), n)
       starts.append(make_binary_from_float(bf,n,seed+len(starts)))
    # random
    for r in range(3): starts.append(random_binary(n,seed+100*r))
    # structured
    for r in range(3): starts.append(structured_binary(n, seed+1000+r))
    # maybe reversed/shifted best after local? We'll update start list dynamically? Could append shifts of current best if improves.
    # Allocate time per start: first 40%, rest 10%?

def calibrate_lag_mode():
   n=5; rng=np.random.RandomState(0); x=np.random.rand(n); x=project...
   idx = 3  # random shift
   p=np.zeros(2*n-1); p[idx]=1
   eps=1e-6
   val0=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')[idx]
   xp=x.copy(); xp[0]+=eps; valp=...
   xp[0]-=2eps; valm=...
   num=(valp-valm)/(2eps)
   for mode in (0,1):
      g=weighted_gradient_p(p,x,n,1.0,mode) # dx 1 for counts
      if abs(g[0]-num)<1e-4: return mode
   # try different component j
   return 0
```

General:
```
def shift_to_idx(k,mode): return L+k if mode==0 else L-k
V[p, shift_to_idx(q-p)] += 1-h[q]  # p-k=q
V[p, shift_to_idx(p-q)] -= h[q]     # p+k=q
```

Build V with mode optimization:
```
def build_V_binary(h,n,mode=None):
  if mode is None: mode=_LAG_MODE
  L=n-1; length=2*L+1
  V=np.zeros((n,length), dtype=np.int16)
  p=np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
  for q in range(n):
     hi=1-h[q]; lo=-h[q]
     if mode==0:
        idx1=L+q-p
        idx2=L+p-q
     else:
        idx1=L-(q-p) # L+p-q
        idx2=L-(p-q) # L+q-p
     V[p, idx1] += hi
     V[p, idx2] += lo
  return V
```
Notice mode1 idx1/idx2 just swapped relative to mode0: idx1 = L+p-q, idx2=L+q-p. But both terms added; since hi may differ from lo, swapping matters. Good.

   # V[p, idx] -= δ for idx1=shift(q-p), idx2=shift(p-q)
```

Calibration implementation:
```
_LAG_MODE=0
def calibrate_lag_mode(n=7):
   global _LAG_MODE
   rng=np.random.RandomState(123)
   x=np.linspace(0.2,0.8,n)
   x=project_feasible(x,n)
   q0=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
   idx=int(q0.argmax())
   p=np.zeros(2*n-1); p[idx]=1.0
   eps=1e-6
   num=np.zeros(n)
   for r in range(n):
      xp=x.copy(); xp[r]+=eps
      qpp=np.correlate(xp,1-xp,'full')[idx]
      xm=x.copy(); xm[r]-=eps
      qmm=np.correlate(xm,1-xm,'full')[idx]
      num[r]=(qpp-qmm)/(2*eps)
   best=1e9; best_mode=0
   for mode in (0,1):
      g=weighted_gradient_p(p,x,n,1.0,mode)
      err=np.linalg.norm(g-num)
      if err<best: best=err; best_mode=mode
   _LAG_MODE=best_mode
   return best_mode
```
Need ensure xp/rxm stay within [0,1]; x in [0.2,0.8], eps ok. Good.

```
def hard_max_polish(h,n,deadline,max_steps=2000):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best=eval_exact(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    dx=2/n; L=n-1; rng=np.random.RandomState(1)
    step=0.05
    for it in range(max_steps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       q=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       M=q.max()
       # top K active: if M>0 choose k = min(20, max(1, int(0.05*n)))? We want near max within tol.
       order=np.argsort(q)[::-1]
       K = min(20, max(3, int(n*0.05)))
       # But if q differences large, top few. Use active_threshold = M - max(1e-4, 1e-3*(M+1))
       active = q >= M - max(1e-8, 1e-3)
       if active.sum()==0: active[order[0]]=True
       K_active=int(active.sum())
       if K_active > 50: active[order[:50]]=True; active[order[50:]] = False? creating bool copy
       p=np.zeros(2*n-1); p[active]=1.0/active.sum()
       grad=weighted_gradient_p(p,h,n,dx)
       d=-grad; d-=d.mean()
       norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if norm<1e-12: break
       d/=norm
       val=best
       accepted=False
       # Backtracking starting step maybe 0.1, shrink to 1e-5
       t=0.1
       for _ in range(12):
           y=h - t*d
           hp=project_feasible(y,n)
           cp=eval_exact(hp,n)
           if cp < best - 1e-14:
               h=hp; best=cp; best_h=hp.copy(); accepted=True; break
           # Armijo: cp < val - 1e-4*t*norm? norm after normalization? Use grad_norm_orig? lost. Use 1e-4*t
           if cp < val - 1e-6*t:
               h=hp; best=cp; best_h=hp.copy(); accepted=True; break
           t *= 0.5
       if not accepted:
           # try a very small random perturbation to unstick
           if it % 50 == 49:
              h=project_feasible(h + 0.01*rng.randn(n), n); best=eval_exact(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
           else:
              break
    return best_h,best
```

Let's write `continuous_refine` with time_deadline and max_alphas list.

```
def continuous_refine(h_start, n, deadline, seed, alphas=None, starts=3):
    h_start = project_feasible(h_start,n)
    best = eval_exact(h_start,n); best_h=h_start.copy()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    if alphas is None: alphas=[30,100,300,1000,3000]
    # First start best_h
    x=best_h
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       _OPT_N=n; _OPT_ALPHA=alpha; _CONSTRAINT_N=n
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':constraint_fun,'jac':constraint_jac}]
       maxiter = 80 if n<=250 else 50
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_objective, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=smooth_gradient, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
          x=project_feasible(res.x,n)
          c=eval_exact(x,n)
          if c<best: best=c; best_h=x
       except Exception: pass
    # perturbed starts with fewer alphas
    for s in range(starts-1):
       if time.time()>deadline-10: break
       scale = 0.03*(s+1)
       x=project_feasible(best_h + scale*rng.randn(n),n)
       c=eval_exact(x,n); update
       for alpha in [30,300,1000]:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          ...
    # maybe active polish if time
    if time.time()+20 < deadline:
       hp,hc=hard_max_polish(best_h,n,deadline-5, max_steps=100)
       if hc<best: best=hc; best_h=hp
    return best_h,best
```
Creating `bounds` each alpha inner; better outside. But constraints list with functions no args. Pass same.

```
def apply_binary_swap(h,V,f,i,j,n,L):
    Vi=V[i]; Vj=V[j]
    f_new = f + Vi - Vj  # f int32 + int16 -> int32? Might cast? yes.
    f_new[L] -= 2
    f_new[L + j - i] += 1
    f_new[L + i - j] += 1
    h[i]=1; h[j]=0
    p=np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    if _LAG_MODE == 0:
        V[p, L + i - p] -= 1
        V[p, L + p - i] -= 1
        V[p, L + j - p] += 1
        V[p, L + p - j] += 1
    else:
        V[p, L + p - i] -= 1
        V[p, L + i - p] -= 1
        V[p, L + p - j] += 1
        V[p, L + j - p] += 1
    # ensure M_new nonnegative? 
    M_new=int(f_new.max())
    return f_new, M_new
```

`binary_local_optimize`:
```
def binary_local_optimize(h,V,f,M,n,deadline,seed,perturb_rounds=3,chunk=64):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    L=n-1
    start=time.time()
    perturb=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        # reduction phase
        while time.time() < deadline:
            res = binary_sweep_once(h,V,f,M,n,L,deadline,rng,chunk)
            if not res[0]: break
            h,V,f,M = res[1:]
        # If no time, break
        if time.time() >= deadline: break
        if perturb >= perturb_rounds: break
        perturb +=1
        # perturb random swaps
        zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
        if zeros.size==0 or ones.size==0: break

        # continue
    return h,V,f,M

def binary_refine(h_start,n,time_budget,seed):
    if n%2!=0: n-=1? But caller even.
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+time_budget
    best_h=None; best_M=10**9; best_c=np.inf
    # create starts
    starts=[]
    b=make_binary_from_float(h_start,n,seed); starts.append(b)
    for s in range(2):
       hf = project_feasible(np.asarray(h_start,dtype=float) + (0.02*(s+1))*np.random.RandomState(seed+100+s).standard_normal(n), n)
       starts.append(make_binary_from_float(hf,n,seed+200+s))
    for s in range(3): starts.append(random_binary(n,seed+500+s))
    for s in range(3): starts.append(structured_binary(n,seed+1000+s))
    # dynamic: if best improves, add shifted/reversed? Could after each start if best_h, add reversed/rotated? Binary rotations may have different boundary but same? Reversal symmetric, rotate not (boundary). But can add `np.roll(best_h, shift)` maybe. We can update best later.
    # time allocation
    first_slice = min(deadline-time.time(), max(20.0, time_budget*0.35))
    if len(starts)>1:
       rest_slice = max(5.0, (time_budget - first_slice)*0.9 / max(1,len(starts)-1))
    # loop
    for idx,bstart in enumerate(starts):
       if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
       if idx==0: sl=first_slice
       else: sl=rest_slice
       if sl > deadline-time.time(): sl = max(5.0, deadline-time.time())
       try:
          h,V,f,M = init_binary_from_b(bstart,n)
          # local optimize
          h,V,f,M = binary_local_optimize(h,V,f,M,n,time.time()+sl,seed+idx, perturb_rounds=3)
          # direct final
          f_chk=...; M=int(f_chk.max())
          M = max? use min? If direct lower than M? use f_chk.
          M = int(f_chk.max())
          h = h.astype(np.int32) # maybe
          c = M*2.0/n
          # it's possible local M was lower but direct f max same; use direct.
          if M<best_M:
              best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
              # optionally add shifted/version of best to starts? We can append and loop? Simpler break? continue.
       except Exception:
          pass
    # if remaining and best_h
    if time.time() < deadline-10 and best_h is not None:
       # continue best binary for remaining
       h,V,f,M = init_binary_from_b(best_h,n)
       h,V,f,M = binary_local_optimize(h,V,f,M,n,deadline, seed+9999, perturb_rounds=5)
       f_chk=...; M=...
       c=...
       update
    return best_h.astype(float) if best_h is not None else ..., best_c, n

Structured binary: Need implement.
```
def structured_binary(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    r1=rng.random_sample(); r2=rng.random_sample(); r3=rng.random_sample()
    if r1<0.5:
       # sine wave
       f=1+int(r2*12)
       phase=r3*2*math.pi
       xf=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*math.pi*f*np.arange(n)/n + phase)
    else:
       # random block lengths
       b=np.zeros(n)
       pos=0
       while pos<n:
           length=int(rng.randint(1, max(2,n//10)))
           val=1 if rng.random()<0.5 else 0
           b[pos:pos+length]=val
           pos+=length
       xf=b
    # adjust density in make_binary_from_float
    return make_binary_from_float(xf,n,seed)
```
Good.

Maybe use `np.concatenate` patterns of 1/0 with random block lengths to create runs.

`project_feasible` robust:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = h[:n] if h.size>n else np.pad(h,(0,n-h.size))
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            rs = room.sum()
            if rs < 1e-14: break
            h = h + (diff / rs) * room
        else:
            hs = h.sum()
            if hs < 1e-14: break
            h = h + (diff / hs) * h
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # exact residual distribution
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 0:
        idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]? To add to values closest to 1? Actually add small residual to those <1. Use order by 1-h descending? If diff small, add to one not 1.
        idx = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0)
        for ii in idx:
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[ii])
            h[ii] += add
            diff -= add
            if diff <= 1e-15: break
    else:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.0)
        for ii in idx:
            sub = min(-diff, h[ii])
            h[ii] -= sub
            diff += sub
            if diff >= -1e-15: break
    return h
```
Adding diff to arbitrary idx okay. It may make h slightly >1 due float; clip after and if sum off? We can leave. For binary target integer, project not used except continuous.

Potential issue: The residual loop for diff positive adds to zeros maybe turning them 1 if diff large; but prior iterations small. Good.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old == n_new: return project_feasible(h_old,n_new)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new,endpoint=False)
    h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.asarray(h_old,dtype=float))
    return project_feasible(h, n_new)
```
If n_old=0? not.

`weighted_gradient_p` implementation with mode:
```
def weighted_gradient_p(p, x, n, dx, mode=None):
    if mode is None: mode=_LAG_MODE
    L=n-1
    W=np.zeros(n, dtype=float); A=np.zeros(n, dtype=float); B=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    # p length 2L+1
    for idx in range(2*L+1):
        pk = float(p[idx])
        if pk <= 1e-15: continue
        if mode == 0:
            k = idx - L
        else:
            k = L - idx
        if k >= 0:
            cnt = n - k
            if cnt > 0:
                W[k:n] += pk
                A[k:n] += pk * x[:cnt]
                B[:cnt] += pk * x[k:]
        else:
            m = -k
            cnt = n - m
            if cnt > 0:
                W[:cnt] += pk
                A[:cnt] += pk * x[m:]
                B[m:n] += pk * x[:cnt]
    return dx*(W - A - B)
```
Check for k positive cnt = n-k. B uses x[k: n-k+k]=x[k:n]. Good. For k=0 cnt=n, B[:n]+=pk*x[0:n]. For k negative m, cnt=n-m, B[m:n] len cnt uses x[:cnt]. Good.
For mode1, k mapping can be from L to -L. Same.

Could optimize by computing p nonzeros: `nz = np.flatnonzero(p > 1e-15)`. Loop over nz. Better:
```
nz = np.where(p > 1e-15)[0]
for idx in nz: ...
```
Yes.

Smooth functions:
```
def smooth_fun_impl(x,n,alpha):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100
    q=np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')
    if q.size==0: return 1e100
    M=float(q.max())
    z=alpha*(q-M)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return M*(2.0/n)
    return (M + math.log(s)/alpha)*(2.0/n)
```
If alpha large and M maybe inf? x finite, q finite.

`smooth_jac_impl`:
```
def smooth_jac_impl(x,n,alpha):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return np.zeros(n)
    q=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    M=float(q.max())
    z=alpha*(q-M)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    s=ez.sum()
    p=np.zeros(q.size)
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p[q == M] = 1.0
        p /= p.sum()
    else:
        p = ez / s
    return weighted_gradient_p(p,x,n,2.0/n)
```
If multiple max and underflow, works.

    n=_CONSTRAINT_N

Now, binary V construction with mode and advanced indexing. Need ensure dtype int16 can handle V entry when both terms same? For p=q,k=0, mode0: idx1=L, idx2=L; V[p,L]+=hi; V[p,L]+=lo; total 1-2h. For h=0 ->1; h=1 -> -1. int16 okay. Updates can temporarily? Should remain -1,0,1. Use int16.
Build loop:
```
def build_V_binary(h,n,mode=None):
    if mode is None: mode=_LAG_MODE
    L=n-1; length=2*L+1
    V=np.zeros((n,length), dtype=np.int16)
    p=np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    for q in range(n):
        hq=int(h[q])
        hi=1-hq
        lo=-hq
        if mode == 0:
            idx1 = L + q - p
            idx2 = L + p - q
        else:
            idx1 = L + p - q
            idx2 = L + q - p
        V[p, idx1] += hi
        V[p, idx2] += lo
    return V
```
If hi or lo 0, still indexing; okay. Could skip if 0 but h random half. Fine.
Memory advanced indexing: `idx1` dtype int64. Good.

`apply_binary_swap` with mode and safety direct maybe not inside for speed. But we can include direct if M_new < M? no.
```
def apply_binary_swap(h,V,f,i,j,n,L):
    Vi = V[i]; Vj=V[j]
    f_new = f + Vi - Vj
    f_new[L] -= 2
    f_new[L + j - i] += 1
    f_new[L + i - j] += 1
    h[i] = 1
    h[j] = 0
    p = np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    if _LAG_MODE == 0:
        V[p, L + i - p] -= 1
        V[p, L + p - i] -= 1
        V[p, L + j - p] += 1
        V[p, L + p - j] += 1
    else:
        V[p, L + p - i] -= 1
        V[p, L + i - p] -= 1
        V[p, L + p - j] += 1
        V[p, L + j - p] += 1
    M_new = int(f_new.max())
    return h,V,f_new,M_new
```
If `f_new` is f + Vi - Vj; f is int32, Vi int16 -> result int32? In NumPy, adding int32 and int16 yields int32. Good. But if f and Vi are views? `f_new` new array. Good.

`binary_sweep_once`: Need `row_idx` for chunk. For each i, if ones size large, chunk. We can choose chunk based on memory: `chunk = max(16, min(len(ones), 128))` maybe. For n=300 len150, one chunk. For n=500 len250, chunk 128 -> 2. For n=800 len400, chunk64 ->7. Good.
```
def binary_sweep_once(h,V,f,M,n,L,deadline,rng,chunk_size=128):
    zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
    ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
    if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0: return False, h,V,f,M
    rng.shuffle(zeros)
    osize = ones.size
    step = chunk_size
    for i in zeros:
       if time.time() > deadline: return False, h,V,f,M
       base = f + V[i]
       for cs in range(0, osize, step):
           ce = min(osize, cs+chunk_size)
           Os = ones[cs:ce]
           if Os.size == 0: continue
           cand = base[None, :] - V[Os]
           cand[:, L] -= 2
           rp = np.arange(Os.size)
           cand[rp, L + Os - i] += 1
           cand[rp, L + i - Os] += 1
           rowmax = cand.max(axis=1)
           minidx = int(np.argmin(rowmax))
           nm = int(rowmax[minidx])
           if nm < M:
               j = int(Os[minidx])
               h,V,f,M = apply_binary_swap(h,V,f,i,j,n,L)
               return True,h,V,f,M
    return False,h,V,f,M
```
Potential performance issue: `base = f + V[i]` creates array; in chunk cand = base[None,:] - V[Os] creates. Good.
If `V[Os]` int16 and base int32, cand int32. `cand[:, L] -= 2` modifies. If `cand` not writeable? It is new.
`rowmax` maybe int32. `minidx` and j.
If no improvement but deadline exceeded, returns False, local loop breaks due time? We can return False; local loop while time check before next.

`binary_local_optimize` with resync:
```
def binary_local_optimize(h,V,f,M,n,deadline,seed,perturb_rounds=3,chunk_size=128):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    L=n-1; swaps=0
    perturb=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       # reduction
       while time.time() < deadline:
           ok,h,V,f,M = binary_sweep_once(...)
           if not ok: break
           swaps += 1
           if swaps % 40 == 0:
               f_chk = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full').astype(np.int32)
               M_chk = int(f_chk.max())
               if M_chk != M or not np.array_equal(f, f_chk):
                   if M_chk < M:
                       h,V,f,M = init_binary_from_b(h,n)? That rebuilds but h int. We can set f=f_chk; M=M_chk; V=build_V_binary(h,n)
                   else:
                       h,V,f,M = init_binary_from_b(h,n) # but init requires b; h is b
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       if perturb >= perturb_rounds: break
       perturb += 1
       zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
       if zeros.size==0 or ones.size==0: break
       k = rng.randint(3,8)
       for _ in range(k):
           if time.time() > deadline: break
           zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=...
           i=int(rng.choice(zeros)); j=int(rng.choice(ones))
           h,V,f,M = apply_binary_swap(...)
       swaps += k
    return h,V,f,M
```
`init_binary_from_b` expects n and b; if called with h (int32) okay. But it recomputes V/f and M. However it also adjusts sum if off (shouldn't). Use only on mismatch.
Potential bug: In resync, if direct M is lower (due to bug where our M too high), we rebuild and continue. Good.

`init_binary_from_b`:
```
def init_binary_from_b(b,n):
    h=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.int32).copy()
    target=n//2
    s=int(h.sum())
    if s != target:
       if s > target:
          ones=np.flatnonzero(h==1)
          h[ones[target:]] = 0  # zero excess? if s-target >1
          # actually ones[s-target:]? zero last s-target
       else:
          zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
          h[zeros[:target-s]] = 1
    V=build_V_binary(h,n)
    f=np.correlate(h.astype(np.int32), (1-h).astype(np.int32), mode='full').astype(np.int32)
    M=int(f.max())
    return h,V,f,M
```
For s>target, `h[ones[s-target:]] =0`? If s-target=2, ones array length s; need zero last 2: `h[ones[-(s-target):]] =0`. Use.
For binary starts sum exactly.

`random_binary`:
```
def random_binary(n,seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   perm=rng.permutation(n)
   b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int32); b[perm[:n//2]]=1; return b
```

`make_binary_from_float`: if n odd? caller even. But if odd and N int? Use int(n/2). We can handle even.
```
def make_binary_from_float(hf,n,seed):
    hf=np.asarray(hf,dtype=float).copy()
    if hf.size<n: pad; elif >n truncate
    target=n//2
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    order=np.argsort(hf + 1e-6*rng.random_sample(n))[::-1]
    b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int32); b[order[:target]]=1
    return b
```

`binary_refine` start composition. Use `np.random`? Use rng.
```
def binary_refine(h_start,n,time_budget,seed):
    if n %2 !=0: n-=1
    if n < 40: return project_feasible(h_start,n), eval_exact(...)
    tstart=time.time()
    deadline=tstart+time_budget
    best_h=None; best_M=10**9; best_c=np.inf
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h_start_f=np.asarray(h_start,dtype=float)
    if len(h_start_f) != n: h_start_f=resample_h(h_start_f,len(h_start_f),n)
    starts=[]
    starts.append(make_binary_from_float(h_start_f,n,seed))
    # perturbed starts
    for s in range(2):
        noisy = project_feasible(h_start_f + (0.05*(s+1))*rng.standard_normal(n), n)
        starts.append(make_binary_from_float(noisy,n,seed+100+s))
    for s in range(3):
        starts.append(random_binary(n, seed+200+s))
    for s in range(3):
        starts.append(structured_binary(n, seed+300+s))
    # Time slices: first 40%, rest 15 each? 
    first = min(deadline - tstart, max(20.0, time_budget*0.35))
    rest_budget = max(0.0, time_budget - first)
    nrest = len(starts)-1
    rest_slice = max(10.0, (rest_budget / max(1,nrest)) * 0.9)
    for idx,b in enumerate(starts):
       if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
       if idx==0: sl=first
       else: sl=rest_slice
       if sl > deadline - time.time(): sl = max(5.0, deadline-time.time())
       if sl < 5: continue
       try:
           h,V,f,M = init_binary_from_b(b,n)
           h,V,f,M = binary_local_optimize(h,V,f,M,n,time.time()+sl, seed+idx, perturb_rounds=2)
           f_chk = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full').astype(np.int32)
           Mint = int(f_chk.max())
           c = Mint*(2.0/n)
           if Mint < best_M:
               best_M=Mint; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
       except Exception: pass
    # if remaining and best
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 30 and best_h is not None:
       try:
          h,V,f,M = init_binary_from_b(best_h,n)
          h,V,f,M = binary_local_optimize(h,V,f,M,n,deadline, seed+999, perturb_rounds=4)
          f_chk=...; Mint=...
          if Mint<best_M: update
       except: pass
    if best_h is None:
       # fallback random binary
       b=random_binary(n,seed)
       h,b? eval
    return best_h.astype(float), best_c, n
```
Potential issue: If a start has M lower but time slice short, it may not reduce fully; but best from direct. Good.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    global _LAG_MODE, _OPT_N, _OPT_ALPHA, _CONSTRAINT_N
    t0=time.time(); total=budget_s
    dl=t0+total-10
    calibrate_lag_mode()
    # parent
    try:
       h_parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n_parent=len(h_parent)
       if n_parent < 20: raise ValueError
    except Exception:
       n_parent=250
       h_parent=np.linspace(0.2,0.8,n_parent)
       h_parent=project_feasible(h_parent,n_parent)
    h_parent=project_feasible(h_parent,n_parent)
    best_h=h_parent.copy(); best_n=n_parent; best_c=eval_exact(best_h,best_n)
    # continuous at parent if n reasonable
    if n_parent <= 400 and time.time() < dl - 100:
        dl1 = t0 + 0.28*(total-10)
        if dl1 > dl: dl1=dl-50
        try:
           h1,c1=continuous_refine(best_h,best_n,dl1,seed)
           if c1<best_c: best_h,h? update
        except: pass
    # continuous resample 300, 350 if time
    cont_n_list=[]
    for n in [300,350]:
       if time.time() + 60 > dl: break
       if n in cont_n_list: continue
       if n == best_n: continue? Maybe continuous already if best_n=300 after parent? parent likely 250. If best_n=n, skip.
       hres=resample_h(best_h,best_n,n)
       # allocate up to 0.10 B each
       dlx = min(dl-50, time.time()+ (total-10)*0.10)
       try:
          h1,c1=continuous_refine(hres,n,dlx,seed+100+n)
          if c1<best_c: update
       except: pass
    # binary
    bin_n_list=[]
    if best_n %2 ==0 and best_n>=80: bin_n_list.append(best_n)
    for n in [250,300,350,400]:
       if n not in bin_n_list and n>=80: bin_n_list.append(n)
    # limit list to top 4
    bin_n_list=bin_n_list[:4]
    # reserve final 60
    for n in bin_n_list:
       if time.time() + 40 > dl: break
       rem_total = dl - time.time() - 60
       if rem_total < 40: break
       # choose budget based on n: maybe 150 for 250,180 for 300,150 for350,100 for400
       if n<=250: intended = min(rem_total*0.3, 180)
       elif n<=300: intended = min(rem_total*0.35, 220)
       elif n<=350: intended = min(rem_total*0.3, 200)
       else: intended = min(rem_total*0.3, 150)
       # But this could use less than rem; next iterations. Simpler: intended = min(rem_total*0.4, 300)
       hstart=resample_h(best_h,best_n,n)
       try:
          hb,cb=binary_refine(hstart,n,min(intended, dl-time.time()-30), seed+n)
          if cb<best_c: best_h=hb; best_c=cb; best_n=n
       except: pass
    # final polish
    rem = dl - time.time()
    if rem > 30:
       if best_n %2 ==0:
          # binary final on best_h at best_n
          try:
             hb,cb=binary_refine(best_h,best_n,max(20.0,min(rem-10,300)), seed+900)
             if cb<best_c: update
          except: pass
       else:
          h1,c1=continuous_refine(best_h,best_n,max(20.0,rem-10), seed+950, alphas=[50,200,1000], starts=2)
          update
    best_h=np.clip(best_h,0,1)
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=eval_exact(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```

Need `run_slsqp` top-level? Could be inside continuous_refine but no closures. We can implement `slsqp_once(x,n,alpha,deadline,maxiter)` top-level.
```
def slsqp_once(x,n,alpha,maxiter):
    global _OPT_N,_OPT_ALPHA,_CONSTRAINT_N
    _OPT_N=n; _OPT_ALPHA=float(alpha); _CONSTRAINT_N=n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':constraint_fun,'jac':constraint_jac}]
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12}
    try:
       res=minimize(smooth_objective,x,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,jac=smooth_gradient,options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return x
    x2=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    return x2
```
No deadline inside; relies maxiter. If SLSQP over deadline, can't stop. Could set maxiter lower. Also can check before calling, but not inside. Accept.

Hard polish: let's implement `hard_max_polish` but simple to not overrun. It uses exact eval many times; set max_steps based on time. If n>300, skip. Could be omitted to save code. But maybe useful. Let's implement a small version:
```
def hard_max_polish(h,n,deadline,max_steps=100):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best=eval_exact(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    if n>400: return best_h,best
    rng=np.random.RandomState(0)
    K=8
    for it in range(max_steps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       q=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       idx=np.argsort(q)[-K:]
       p=np.zeros(q.size); p[idx]=1.0/K
       grad=weighted_gradient_p(p,h,n,2.0/n)
       d=-grad; d-=d.mean(); norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if norm<1e-13: break
       d/=norm
       accepted=False
       t=0.05
       for _ in range(10):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          y=h - t*d
          hp=project_feasible(y,n)
          cp=eval_exact(hp,n)
          if cp < best - 1e-14:
             h=hp; best=cp; best_h=hp.copy(); accepted=True; break
          if cp < best - 1e-8*t:
             h=hp; best=cp; best_h=hp.copy(); accepted=True; break
          t*=0.5
       if not accepted:
          if it%20==19:
             h=project_feasible(h + 0.01*rng.standard_normal(n),n)
             best=eval_exact(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
          else: break
    return best_h,best
```
This may sometimes increase after active change; line search small. Could help. Use only final if time >20s.

```
def calibrate_lag_mode():
    global _LAG_MODE
    n=7
    x=np.linspace(0.2,0.8,n)
    x=project_feasible(x,n)
    q=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    idx=int(np.argmax(q))
    eps=1e-6
    num=np.zeros(n)
    for r in range(n):
       xp=x.copy(); xp[r]+=eps
       qpp=float(np.correlate(xp,1.0-xp,'full')[idx])
       xm=x.copy(); xm[r]-=eps
       qmm=float(np.correlate(xm,1.0-xm,'full')[idx])
       num[r]=(qpp-qmm)/(2.0*eps)
    best_err=1e100; best_mode=0
    for mode in (0,1):
       p=np.zeros(q.size); p[idx]=1.0
       g=weighted_gradient_p(p,x,n,1.0,mode)
       err=float(np.linalg.norm(g-num))
       if err < best_err: best_err=err; best_mode=mode
    _LAG_MODE = best_mode
    return best_mode
```
If x at bounds? 0.2 to0.8 eps fine. project might alter but still interior.

Potential issue: finite difference with eps=1e-6 and q values O(1); error ~1e-12, okay. Use central.

Let's test gradient mode with k positive/negative? Calibration at index argmax maybe one shift. It only checks one shift; if mapping/order same for all and sign correct. If the wrong mode gives similar? It should differ. Good.

Now, binary V using mode from calibration. If mode wrong but calibration maybe fails, binary may fail. But max still from direct f for best; it won't return incorrect low. It may not improve.

Could we avoid relying on binary V mode by using direct f updates with `np.roll`? Alternative: For each swap, compute delta via shifts using h independent of mode, and use direct f? We can derive delta for actual output by using two possible formulas and direct? Too complex. Calibration okay.

Let's double-check weighted_gradient ranges for mode1. Suppose mode1 maps idx to k=L-idx. For idx0, k=L positive largest; for idx2L, k=-L. Branch uses k positive. Good.

Now, possible performance of `weighted_gradient_p`: loops over p nonzeros. For softmax with α low, all 499 nonzero. Each branch does slice adds. For n=250, 499*~125 = 62k operations but in Python 499 loops with slice addition length up to250. Each slice addition creates? It adds in-place? `W[k:n] += pk` uses ufunc, okay. 499 loops per gradient. 1000 gradients ->500k loops, maybe few seconds. Good.
For n=350, 699 loops, slice length ~350 ->250k per gradient; 500 gradients ->125M element ops, okay maybe 10s.

Could optimize by using FFT? Not needed.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_LAG_MODE = 0
_OPT_N = 0
_OPT_ALPHA = 100.0
_CONSTRAINT_N = 0


def eval_exact(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        return 1e100
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size > n:
            h = h[:n]
        else:
            h = np.pad(h, (0, n - h.size), mode="edge")
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(120):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            rs = float(room.sum())
            if rs < 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + (diff / rs) * room
        else:
            hs = float(h.sum())
            if hs < 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + (diff / hs) * h

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if diff > 0:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0)
        for ii in idx:
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[ii])
            h[ii] += add
            diff -= add
            if diff <= 1e-15:
                break
    elif diff < 0:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.0)
        for ii in idx:
            sub = min(-diff, h[ii])
            h[ii] -= sub
            diff += sub
            if diff >= -1e-15:
                break
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def resample_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    h_old = np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float)
    if n_old <= 0:
        return np.full(n_new, 0.5)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_feasible(h_old, n_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
    return project_feasible(out, n_new)


def make_binary_from_float(hf, n, seed=0):
    hf = np.asarray(hf, dtype=float).copy()
    if hf.size != n:
        if hf.size > n:
            hf = hf[:n]
        else:
            hf = np.pad(hf, (0, n - hf.size), mode="edge")
    target = int(n // 2)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    order = np.argsort(hf + 1e-8 * rng.random_sample(n))[::-1]
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    b[order[:target]] = 1
    return b


def random_binary(n, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    perm = rng.permutation(n)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    b[perm[: n // 2]] = 1
    return b


def structured_binary(n, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    r = rng.random_sample()
    i = np.arange(n)
    if r < 0.6:
        freq = 1 + int(rng.random_sample() * 14)
        phase = 2.0 * math.pi * rng.random_sample()
        xf = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * i / float(n) + phase)
    else:
        xf = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        pos = 0
        while pos < n:
            ln = max(1, int(rng.randint(1, max(2, n // 12))))
            val = 1.0 if rng.random_sample() < 0.5 else 0.0
            xf[pos:pos + ln] = val
            pos += ln
    return make_binary_from_float(xf, n, seed)


def build_V_binary(h, n, mode=None):
    if mode is None:
        mode = _LAG_MODE
    L = n - 1
    length = 2 * L + 1
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int32)
    V = np.zeros((n, length), dtype=np.int16)
    p = np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    for q in range(n):
        hq = int(h[q])
        hi = 1 - hq
        lo = -hq
        if mode == 0:
            idx1 = L + q - p
            idx2 = L + p - q
        else:
            idx1 = L + p - q
            idx2 = L + q - p
        V[p, idx1] += hi
        V[p, idx2] += lo
    return V


def init_binary_from_b(b, n):
    h = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.int32).copy()
    target = int(n // 2)
    s = int(h.sum())
    if s != target:
        if s > target:
            ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
            h[ones[-(s - target):]] = 0
        else:
            zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
            h[zeros[:target - s]] = 1
    V = build_V_binary(h, n)
    f = np.correlate(h, (1 - h).astype(np.int32), mode="full").astype(np.int32)
    M = int(f.max())
    return h, V, f, M


def apply_binary_swap(h, V, f, i, j, n):
    L = n - 1
    f_new = f + V[i] - V[j]
    f_new[L] -= 2
    f_new[L + j - i] += 1
    f_new[L + i - j] += 1

    h[i] = 1
    h[j] = 0

    p = np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    if _LAG_MODE == 0:
        V[p, L + i - p] -= 1
        V[p, L + p - i] -= 1
        V[p, L + j - p] += 1
        V[p, L + p - j] += 1
    else:
        V[p, L + p - i] -= 1
        V[p, L + i - p] -= 1
        V[p, L + p - j] += 1
        V[p, L + j - p] += 1

    M_new = int(f_new.max())
    return h, V, f_new, M_new


def binary_sweep_once(h, V, f, M, n, deadline, rng, chunk_size=128):
    L = n - 1
    zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
    ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
    if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
        return False, h, V, f, M

    rng.shuffle(zeros)
    osize = int(ones.size)

    for i in zeros:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        base = f + V[i]
        for cs in range(0, osize, chunk_size):
            ce = min(osize, cs + chunk_size)
            Os = ones[cs:ce]
            if Os.size == 0:
                continue
            cand = base[None, :] - V[Os]
            cand[:, L] -= 2
            rp = np.arange(Os.size)
            cand[rp, L + Os - i] += 1
            cand[rp, L + i - Os] += 1
            rowmax = cand.max(axis=1)
            mi = int(np.argmin(rowmax))
            nm = int(rowmax[mi])
            if nm < M:
                j = int(Os[mi])
                h, V, f, M = apply_binary_swap(h, V, f, i, j, n)
                return True, h, V, f, M
    return False, h, V, f, M


def binary_local_optimize(h, V, f, M, n, deadline, seed=0, perturb_rounds=3, chunk_size=128):
    L = n - 1
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    swaps = 0
    perturb = 0

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_M = int(M)

    while time.time() < deadline:
        while time.time() < deadline:
            ok, h, V, f, M = binary_sweep_once(
                h, V, f, M, n, deadline, rng, chunk_size
            )
            if not ok:
                break
            swaps += 1
            if M < best_M:
                best_M = int(M)
                best_h = h.copy()

            if swaps % 40 == 0:
                f_chk = np.correlate(h, (1 - h).astype(np.int32), mode="full").astype(np.int32)
                M_chk = int(f_chk.max())
                if M_chk != M or not np.array_equal(f, f_chk):
                    h, V, f, M = init_binary_from_b(h, n)
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = int(M)
                    best_h = h.copy()

        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break

        if perturb >= perturb_rounds:
            break
        perturb += 1

        zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
        ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
        if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
            break

        for _ in range(int(rng.randint(3, 8))):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
            ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
            if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
                break
            i = int(rng.choice(zeros))
            j = int(rng.choice(ones))
            h, V, f, M = apply_binary_swap(h, V, f, i, j, n)
            swaps += 1

        if M < best_M:
            best_M = int(M)
            best_h = h.copy()

    return h, V, f, M, best_h, best_M


def binary_refine(h_start, n, time_budget, seed=0):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n -= 1
    if n < 40:
        hf = project_feasible(np.asarray(h_start, dtype=float), n)
        return hf, eval_exact(hf, n), n

    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, float(time_budget))

    h_start_f = np.asarray(h_start, dtype=float)
    if h_start_f.size != n:
        h_start_f = resample_h(h_start_f, h_start_f.size, n)

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    starts = []
    starts.append(make_binary_from_float(h_start_f, n, seed))

    for s in range(2):
        noisy = project_feasible(h_start_f + (0.05 * (s + 1)) * rng.standard_normal(n), n)
        starts.append(make_binary_from_float(noisy, n, seed + 100 + s))

    for s in range(3):
        starts.append(random_binary(n, seed + 200 + s))

    for s in range(3):
        starts.append(structured_binary(n, seed + 300 + s))

    best_h = None
    best_M = 10 ** 9
    best_c = 1e100

    first_slice = min(deadline - time.time(), max(20.0, float(time_budget) * 0.35))
    rest_budget = max(0.0, float(time_budget) - first_slice)
    rest_slice = max(8.0, (rest_budget / max(1, len(starts) - 1)) * 0.9)

    for idx, b in enumerate(starts):
        if time.time() > deadline - 2:
            break
        if idx == 0:
            slice_time = first_slice
        else:
            slice_time = rest_slice
        slice_time = max(5.0, min(slice_time, deadline - time.time() - 2))
        if slice_time < 5.0:
            continue

        try:
            h, V, f, M = init_binary_from_b(b, n)
            h, V, f, M, bh, bM = binary_local_optimize(
                h, V, f, M, n, time.time() + slice_time,
                seed + idx, perturb_rounds=2
            )
            f_chk = np.correlate(bh, (1 - bh).astype(np.int32), mode="full").astype(np.int32)
            mint = int(f_chk.max())
            cval = mint * (2.0 / float(n))
            if mint < best_M:
                best_M = mint
                best_h = bh.copy()
                best_c = cval
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 30:
        try:
            h, V, f, M = init_binary_from_b(best_h, n)
            h, V, f, M, bh, bM = binary_local_optimize(
                h, V, f, M, n, deadline, seed + 9999,
                perturb_rounds=4
            )
            f_chk = np.correlate(bh, (1 - bh).astype(np.int32), mode="full").astype(np.int32)
            mint = int(f_chk.max())
            cval = mint * (2.0 / float(n))
            if mint < best_M:
                best_M = mint
                best_h = bh.copy()
                best_c = cval
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is None:
        b = random_binary(n, seed)
        h = b.astype(float)
        return h, eval_exact(h, n), n

    return best_h.astype(float), float(best_c), n


def weighted_gradient_p(p, x, n, dx, mode=None):
    if mode is None:
        mode = _LAG_MODE
    n = int(n)
    L = n - 1
    W = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    A = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    B = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    nz = np.flatnonzero(p > 1e-15)

    for idx in nz:
        pk = float(p[idx])
        if pk <= 1e-15:
            continue
        if mode == 0:
            k = int(idx) - L
        else:
            k = L - int(idx)

        if k >= 0:
            cnt = n - k
            if cnt > 0:
                W[k:n] += pk
                A[k:n] += pk * x[:cnt]
                B[:cnt] += pk * x[k:]
        else:
            m = -k
            cnt = n - m
            if cnt > 0:
                W[:cnt] += pk
                A[:cnt] += pk * x[m:]
                B[m:n] += pk * x[:cnt]

    return dx * (W - A - B)


def smooth_fun_impl(x, n, alpha):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size != int(n) or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100
    q = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    if q.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    M = float(np.max(q))
    z = float(alpha) * (q - M)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return M * (2.0 / float(n))
    return (M + math.log(s) / float(alpha)) * (2.0 / float(n))


def smooth_jac_impl(x, n, alpha):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    q = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    M = float(np.max(q))
    z = float(alpha) * (q - M)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    p = np.zeros(q.size, dtype=float)
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p[q == M] = 1.0
        p /= max(1.0, p.sum())
    else:
        p = ez / s
    return weighted_gradient_p(p, x, n, 2.0 / float(n))


def smooth_objective(x):
    return smooth_fun_impl(x, _OPT_N, _OPT_ALPHA)


def smooth_gradient(x):
    return smooth_jac_impl(x, _OPT_N, _OPT_ALPHA)


def constraint_fun(x):
    n = int(_CONSTRAINT_N)
    return float(np.sum(np.asarray(x, dtype=float)) - 0.5 * float(n))


def constraint_jac(x):
    n = int(_CONSTRAINT_N)
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def slsqp_once(x, n, alpha, maxiter):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_ALPHA, _CONSTRAINT_N
    _OPT_N = int(n)
    _OPT_ALPHA = float(alpha)
    _CONSTRAINT_N = int(n)

    x = project_feasible(x, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons_with_jac = [
        {"type": "eq", "fun": constraint_fun, "jac": constraint_jac}
    ]
    cons_simple = [
        {"type": "eq", "fun": constraint_fun}
    ]
    opts = {"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-8}

    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_objective,
            x,
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=smooth_gradient,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons_with_jac,
            options=opts
        )
        out = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        return out
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_objective,
            x,
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=smooth_gradient,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons_simple,
            options=opts
        )
        return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        return project_feasible(x, n)


def hard_max_polish(h, n, deadline, max_steps=40):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = eval_exact(h, n)
    if n > 400:
        return best_h, best_c

    rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
    K = min(8, 2 * n - 1)

    for _ in range(int(max_steps)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        q = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        idx = np.argsort(q)[-K:]
        p = np.zeros(q.size, dtype=float)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
        g = weighted_gradient_p(p, h, n, 2.0 / float(n))
        d = -g
        d -= d.mean()
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if norm < 1e-13:
            break
        d /= norm

        accepted = False
        t = 0.05
        for _ in range(12):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            y = h - t * d
            hp = project_feasible(y, n)
            cp = eval_exact(hp, n)
            if cp < best_c - 1e-14 or cp < best_c - 1e-9 * t:
                h = hp
                best_c = cp
                best_h = hp.copy()
                accepted = True
                break
            t *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            if rng.random_sample() < 0.15:
                h = project_feasible(h + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n), n)
                best_c = eval_exact(h, n)
                best_h = h.copy()
            else:
                break

    return best_h, best_c


def continuous_refine(h_start, n, deadline, seed=0, alphas=None, extra_starts=2):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h_start, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = eval_exact(h, n)

    if n < 20 or n > 500 or time.time() > deadline:
        return best_h, best_c

    if alphas is None:
        if n <= 250:
            alphas = [20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1000.0]
        else:
            alphas = [50.0, 200.0, 800.0]

    maxiter = 60 if n <= 250 else 35

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 3:
            break
        x = slsqp_once(best_h, n, alpha, maxiter)
        c = eval_exact(x, n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = x.copy()

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for s in range(int(extra_starts)):
        if time.time() > deadline - 5:
            break
        scale = 0.02 * (s + 1)
        x0 = project_feasible(best_h + scale * rng.standard_normal(n), n)
        local_x = x0.copy()
        local_c = eval_exact(local_x, n)
        if local_c < best_c:
            best_c = local_c
            best_h = local_x.copy()

        for alpha in [80.0, 800.0]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 3:
                break
            x = slsqp_once(local_x, n, alpha, max(25, maxiter - 15))
            c = eval_exact(x, n)
            if c < local_c:
                local_x = x.copy()
                local_c = c
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = x.copy()

    if time.time() + 12 < deadline and n <= 350:
        hp, hc = hard_max_polish(best_h, n, deadline - 2, max_steps=35)
        if hc < best_c:
            best_c = hc
            best_h = hp.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def calibrate_lag_mode():
    global _LAG_MODE
    n = 7
    x = np.linspace(0.2, 0.8, n)
    x = project_feasible(x, n)
    q = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    idx = int(np.argmax(q))
    eps = 1e-6
    num = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for r in range(n):
        xp = x.copy()
        xp[r] += eps
        qpp = float(np.correlate(xp, 1.0 - xp, mode="full")[idx])
        xm = x.copy()
        xm[r] -= eps
        qmm = float(np.correlate(xm, 1.0 - xm, mode="full")[idx])
        num[r] = (qpp - qmm) / (2.0 * eps)

    best_err = 1e100
    best_mode = 0
    p = np.zeros(q.size, dtype=float)
    p[idx] = 1.0
    for mode in (0, 1):
        g = weighted_gradient_p(p, x, n, 1.0, mode)
        err = float(np.linalg.norm(g - num))
        if err < best_err:
            best_err = err
            best_mode = mode
    _LAG_MODE = int(best_mode)
    return _LAG_MODE


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = max(20.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)

    try:
        calibrate_lag_mode()
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        h_parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_parent = int(len(h_parent))
        if n_parent < 20:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        n_parent = 250
        h_parent = np.linspace(0.2, 0.8, n_parent)
        h_parent = project_feasible(h_parent, n_parent)

    h_parent = project_feasible(h_parent, n_parent)
    best_h = h_parent.copy()
    best_n = n_parent
    best_c = eval_exact(best_h, best_n)

    if best_n <= 450 and time.time() + 80 < deadline:
        dl1 = t0 + 0.28 * B
        dl1 = min(dl1, deadline - 80)
        if dl1 > time.time() + 20:
            try:
                h1, c1 = continuous_refine(best_h, best_n, dl1, seed)
                if c1 < best_c:
                    best_h = h1.copy()
                    best_c = c1
            except Exception:
                pass

    for nnew in (300, 350):
        if time.time() + 50 > deadline:
            break
        if nnew == best_n:
            continue
        try:
            hres = resample_h(best_h, best_n, nnew)
            dlx = min(deadline - 80, time.time() + 0.09 * B)
            if dlx <= time.time() + 15:
                continue
            h1, c1 = continuous_refine(hres, nnew, dlx, seed + 100 + nnew, extra_starts=1)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h = h1.copy()
                best_c = c1
                best_n = nnew
        except Exception:
            pass

    bin_n_list = []
    even_best = best_n if (best_n % 2 == 0 and best_n >= 80) else None
    if even_best is not None:
        bin_n_list.append(even_best)
    for n in (250, 300, 350, 400):
        if n not in bin_n_list:
            bin_n_list.append(n)
    bin_n_list = bin_n_list[:4]

    for idx, n in enumerate(bin_n_list):
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.time() - 45)
        if rem < 35:
            break
        intended = rem / float(len(bin_n_list) - idx)
        intended = min(intended, 350.0)
        if intended < 20.0:
            break
        try:
            hstart = resample_h(best_h, best_n, n)
            hb, cb = binary_refine(hstart, n, min(intended, deadline - time.time() - 20), seed + 17 * n)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h = np.asarray(hb, dtype=float)
                best_c = cb
                best_n = n
        except Exception:
            pass

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
    if rem > 35:
        if best_n % 2 == 0:
            try:
                hb, cb = binary_refine(best_h, best_n, max(25.0, rem - 20), seed + 911)
                if cb < best_c:
                    best_h = np.asarray(hb, dtype=float)
                    best_c = cb
                    best_n = best_n
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            try:
                h1, c1 = continuous_refine(best_h, best_n, max(20.0, rem - 15), seed + 951, extra_starts=1)
                if c1 < best_c:
                    best_h = h1.copy()
                    best_c = c1
            except Exception:
                pass

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = eval_exact(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
